</div></section> <div class="pb-container"> </div></div> <script>(function(){window.pageBuilder=window.pageBuilder||{};var testIdToFeatureId={};var status={};var TIMEOUT=1500;var TIME_NOW=Date.now();var BANDITO_TESTS_API="/bandito/tests-pb/?q\x3d";var BANDITO_PERF_API="https://bandito-events-prod.infobae.arcpublishing.com/api/save-perf";var BANDITO_EVENTS_API="https://bandito-events-prod.infobae.arcpublishing.com/api/save-event";function ajax(url,success,failure,timeout){var xhr=new XMLHttpRequest;xhr.open("GET",url);xhr.onreadystatechange=function(){if(xhr.readyState==
4)if(xhr.status===200)try{var response=JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);success(response)}catch(e){failure()}else failure()};if(timeout){xhr.ontimeout=failure;xhr.timeout=timeout}xhr.send()}function perf(status){var elapsed=Date.now()-TIME_NOW;var url=BANDITO_PERF_API+"?status\x3d"+status+"\x26time\x3d"+elapsed;ajax(url,function(){},function(){})}function showDefaultVariants(){for(var id in _variantFeatureIds)if(_variantFeatureIds.hasOwnProperty(id)){perf("failure");showDefaultVariant(_variantFeatureIds[id],
false)}}function showDefaultVariant(testId,success){var feature=document.getElementById(testIdToFeatureId[testId]);variantLoaded(testId,testId,feature,success)}function showOtherVariant(featureId,testId,winner,timeLeft){var feature=document.getElementById(featureId);var uri=_context+"/api/v2/render/feature/variant/"+winner+"?rid\x3d"+_rid+"\x26uri\x3d"+_uri+"\x26outputType\x3d"+_outputType;ajax(uri,function(response){var resources=response.pageResources||{};var parent=feature.parentNode;var div=document.createElement("div");
div.innerHTML=response.rendering;var newFeature=div.childNodes[0];parent.replaceChild(newFeature,feature);var scripts=Array.prototype.slice.call(newFeature.getElementsByTagName("script"));for(var i=0;i<scripts.length;i++)if(scripts[i].src!=""){var s=document.createElement("script");s.setAttribute("src",scripts[i].src);document.body.appendChild(s)}else eval(scripts[i].innerHTML);for(var file in resources)if(resources.hasOwnProperty(file)&&file.endsWith(".js")){var script=document.createElement("script");
script.setAttribute("src",resources[file]);document.body.appendChild(script)}perf("success-variant");variantLoaded(testId,winner,newFeature,true)},function(){perf("failure-step2");variantLoaded(testId,testId,feature,false)},timeLeft)}function variantLoaded(testId,variantId,feature,success){if(!status[testId]){status[testId]=true;feature.style.visibility="visible";if(success){var request=new XMLHttpRequest;request.open("POST",BANDITO_EVENTS_API,true);request.setRequestHeader("Content-Type","application/json");
request.send(JSON.stringify({event:"served",test_id:testId,variant_id:variantId}));feature.querySelectorAll("a").forEach(function(el){el.addEventListener("click",function(event){if(event&&event.target&&event.target.href){var request=new XMLHttpRequest;request.open("POST",BANDITO_EVENTS_API,true);request.setRequestHeader("Content-Type","application/json");request.send(JSON.stringify({event:"clicked",test_id:testId,variant_id:variantId}));if(event.button>0||event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey||event.shiftKey);
